**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/append-data.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/append-data.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_layer", "label_zh": "輸入圖層", "label_en": "Input Layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Record Set", "description_en": "The hosted feature layer to which features will be appended."}, {"parameter": "append_layer", "label_zh": "追加圖層", "label_en": "Append Layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Record Set", "description_en": "The layer containing features that will be appended to the input layer."}, {"parameter": "append_method", "label_zh": "追加方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Append Method", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how fields from the input layer will be appended with values from the append layer. Append matching fields only—Input layer fields will only be appended if they have a matching field in the append layer. Fields without a match will be appended with null values. Append matching fields and resolve differences—Input layer fields can be appended with append layer fields of the same name and different type or with values calculated from Arcade expressions."}, {"parameter": "append_fields", "label_zh": "追加欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Append Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The append layer fields of the same type and different name as the input layer fields that will be appended. Select the input field to append to, and the append field containing the values to append. Value table columns: Input Field—The input layer field to match with an append layer field. Append Field—The append layer field to match with an input layer field."}, {"parameter": "append_expressions", "label_zh": "追加表達式 （可選）", "label_en": "Append Expressions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The Arcade expression that will be used to calculate field values for the input field. Expressions are written in Arcade and can include mathematical operators and multiple fields. Select the fields to append to, and provide an expression for each to calculate the values to append. If the layer is added to the map, the fields and helpers can be used to build an expression. Value table columns: Input Field—The input layer field to append with the calculated Arcade expression values. Expression—The Arcade expression that will be used to calculate field values for the input field."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.geoanalytics.AppendData(input_layer, append_layer, append_method, {append_fields}, {append_expressions})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_layer、append_layer、append_method、append_fields、append_expressions。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-2

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**text**　This tool modifies the input data. See Tools that modify or update the input data for more information and strategies to avoid undesired data changes.
The following are supported feature attribute metrics: Length—The default attributes are LEG, LEN, LEN_, LGN, and LZN. Width—The default attributes are WID, WID_, and WGP. Area—The default attributes are ARA, ARE, and ARE_. Angle of orientation—The default attributes are AOO, DOF, and FEO. Elevation—The default attributes are ZV2 and ZVH (must be z-enabled data). 注: Elevation metrics that the tool derives from a raster file specified for the Elevation Raster parameter value will overwrite existing elevation data. Military Grid Reference System—The default attributes are MGRSValue and MGRS.
Input metric types are processed based on the geometry of the feature class as follows: Points—Elevation Lines—Length and Elevation Polygons—Length, Width, Area, Angle of Orientation, and Elevation

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#usage

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 注意：

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-3

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**text**　The Input Length Attributes, Input Width Attributes, Input Area Attributes, Input Angle of Orientation Attributes, and Input Elevation Attributes parameter values reference a comma-delimited list of field names in the data model. If one or more fields exist in the Input Features parameter value, the field will be calculated according to the metrics specified for the Input Metric Types parameter value.
You can add custom field names to the list of options in the Input Length Attributes, Input Width Attributes, Input Area Attributes, Input Angle of Orientation Attributes, and Input Elevation Attributes parameter values. This tool will attempt to compute metrics in a custom field.
This tool can calculate the Military Grid Reference System (MGRS) coordinates for features using the standard precision formats as well as the format used for Image City Map (ICM) annotation features. For ICM format, the value is a combination of the column and row number in which the feature is located. 許可： The ArcGIS Spatial Analyst extension allows this tool to rotate features such as springs and natural pools to face downhill. The ArcGIS Topographic Mapping product data files include certain point feature layers with a CBO_Rotation attribute field that is set up to be updated by this tool using the Elevation Raster parameter value and specifying Angle of orientation for the Input Metric Types parameter value.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#usage

- Input Metric Types

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-13

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**parameter**　mgrs_attributes

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Input MGRS Attributes （可選）
- A comma-delimited string of field names from which the MGRS coordinates will be calculated. The default is MGRSValue,MGRS. You can add the names of other MGRS fields; if the fields exist in the Input Features value, they will be computed. The fields must have a String field type and a field length greater than the largest possible MGRS coordinate value.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 參數

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-14

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**parameter**　mgrs_precision

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- MGRS Precision （可選）
- Specifies the precision of the coordinates that will be calculated for the target attributes. 6×8 (4Q)—The precision will be calculated at grid-level precision, typically the polygon formed by a 6-degree wide UTM zone and 8-degree high latitude bands. 100km (4QFJ)—The precision will be calculated at 100,000 meters squared. 10km (4QFJ16)—The precision will be calculated at 10,000 meters squared. 1km (4QFJ1267)—The precision will be calculated at 1,000 meters squared. 100m (4QFJ123678)—The precision will be calculated at 100 meters squared. 10m (4QFJ12346789)—The precision will be calculated at 10 meters squared. 1m (4QFJ1234567890)—The precision will be calculated at 1 meter squared. Image City Map (1234)—The precision will be calculated at the level of an Image City Map (ICM). This is the default.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-15

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**parameter**　elevation_raster

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Elevation Raster （可選）
- The raster file from which the elevation metrics will be derived. Elevation metrics derived from a raster file specified here will overwrite existing elevation data. 許可： The Spatial Analyst extension is required when using an elevation raster and the angle of orientation to rotate point features so that they face downhill.
- Raster Layer; Mosaic Layer

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**

- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**parameter**　only_populate_null_values

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Only populate NULL values （可選）
- Specifies whether only null metric values will be calculated. 選中—Only null values will be calculated. 未選中—All values will be calculated. This option will overwrite the existing values for the specified input attribute fields of the Input Features parameter value. This is the default.
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 派生輸出

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-20

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**parameter**　in_metric_types

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- in_metric_types [in_metric_types,...]
- Specifies the types of metrics that will be calculated. 許可： The Spatial Analyst extension is required when using an elevation raster and the ANGLE_OF_ORIENTATION option to rotate point features so that they face downhill. LENGTH—Length metrics will be calculated. WIDTH—Width metrics will be calculated. AREA—Area metrics will be calculated. ANGLE_OF_ORIENTATION—Angle of orientation metrics will be calculated. ELEVATION—Elevation metrics will be calculated. MGRS—Military Grid Reference System coordinates will be calculated.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#derived-output

- only_populate_null_values （可選）
- Specifies whether only null metric values will be calculated. ONLYPOPNULLVALS—Only null values will be calculated. NO_ONLYPOPNULLVALS—All values will be calculated. This option will overwrite the existing values for the specified input attribute fields of the in_features parameter value. This is the default.
- Boolean

**text**　# Name: CalculateMetrics_sample1.py
# Description: Calculating the width field for features in a polyline feature class

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting Local Variables
input_features = r'C:\Temp\Test.gdb\RiverLines'
field_name = 'WID'

# Creating feature layer
river_lines = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(input_features, 'RiverLines').getOutput(0)

# Calculating WIDTH of input features on the WID field
arcpy.topographic.CalculateMetrics(river_lines, 'WIDTH', '#', field_name, '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', 'NO_ONLYPOPNULLVALS')

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-35

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**text**　# Name: CalculateMetrics_sample2.py
# Description: Iterates through the features in a database and calculates metric attributes

# Import System Modules
import arcpy
import string
import os

# Setting Local Variables
input_database = r'C:\Data\Sample.gdb'
raster = r'C:\Data\Monterey.dt2'
metric_type = 'LENGTH;WIDTH;AREA;ANGLE_OF_ORIENTATION;ELEVATION;MGRS'

# Getting feature classes from database
arcpy.env.workspace = input_database
feature_classes = []
feature_datasets = arcpy.ListDatasets('*', 'Feature')
for dataset in feature_datasets:
    fclass = arcpy.ListFeatureClasses('*', '', dataset)
    for fc in fclass:
        feature_classes.append(os.path.join(input_database, dataset, fc))

# Converting feature classes into layers
layer_list = []
for feat_class in feature_classes:
    class_name = os.path.split(feat_class)[1]
    arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(feat_class, class_name)
    layer_list.append(class_name)

# Creating string for input to Calculate Metrics Fields tool
feature_string = ''
for layer in layer_list:
    feature_string += '{};'.format(layer)

# Calculating Metric fields on all features classes in the input database
arcpy.topographic.CalculateMetrics(feature_string, metric_type, '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', '#', raster, 'NO_ONLYPOPNULLVALS')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#derived-output

## Calculate Metrics (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 資料管理工具集
- 圖徵工具集

**english**　Calculate Metrics

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html

**id**　0302d71b47d4239846-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/calculate-metrics.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Calculate Metrics，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Features [in_features]、Input Metric Types [in_metric_types]、Input Length Attributes （可選） [in_length_attributes]、Input Width Attributes （可選） [in_width_attributes]、Input Area Attributes （可選） [in_area_attributes]、Input Angle of Orientation Attributes （可選） [in_angle_attributes]、Input Elevation Attributes （可選） [in_elevation_attributes]、Input Floating Point Precision （可選） [in_precision]、Input MGRS Attributes （可選） [mgrs_attributes]、MGRS Precision （可選） [mgrs_precision]、Elevation Raster （可選） [elevation_raster]、Only populate NULL values （可選） [only_populate_null_values]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單
